				4065 = { dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4065 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 1
					}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 2
					}}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 3
					}}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4065 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel1
							duration = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				4065 = { dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4065 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 2
					}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 3
					}}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4065 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel2
							duration = -1
						}
						remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				4065 = { dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4065 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel
						tier = 3
					}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4065 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel3
							duration = -1
						}
						remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					4065 = { dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel_modifier_trigger = no }
					4065 = {
						owner = {
							has_country_flag = dependencylost
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel1
				remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel2
				remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = subjectmonumentlost
				OR ={
					has_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel1
					has_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel2
					has_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel3
				}
			}
			remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel1
			remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel2
			remove_country_modifier = dwarovar_marrhold_tunnel3
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				4931 = { yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4931 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 1
					}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 2
					}}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 3
					}}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4931 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring1
							duration = -1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				4931 = { yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4931 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 2
					}
					NOT = { has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 3
					}}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4931 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring2
							duration = -1
						}
						remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring1
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				4931 = { yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring_modifier_trigger = yes }
				4931 = {
					has_great_project = {
						type = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring
						tier = 3
					}
					owner = {
						is_subject = yes
						NOT = { is_subject_of_type = tributary_state }
					}
				}
			}
			4931 = { 
				owner = { 
					overlord = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring3
							duration = -1
						}
						remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring2
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					4931 = { yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring_modifier_trigger = no }
					4931 = {
						owner = {
							has_country_flag = dependencylost
						}
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring1
				remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring2
				remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = subjectmonumentlost
				OR ={
					has_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring1
					has_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring2
					has_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring3
				}
			}
			remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring1
			remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring2
			remove_country_modifier = yinquan_the_conquerors_hotspring3
		}
	}
}
